---

## PMS — Performance Management System

### Architecture
Two separate backends + one frontend:

1. **`backend/app/`** — SQLite-based PMS backend (FastAPI + SQLite)
2. **`backend/gms-backend/`** — PostgreSQL-based GMS backend (FastAPI + PostgreSQL + Alembic) — this is the **main/active** backend
3. **`frontend/`** — React + Vite + Tailwind CSS SPA

---

### GMS Backend (main)

**Tech stack:** FastAPI, SQLAlchemy 2.0, PostgreSQL (psycopg3), Alembic, JWT (python-jose), bcrypt, Pydantic v2

**Models:**
- `User` — id, email, name, password_hash, role (admin/manager/member), manager_id (self-ref FK), team_id
- `Team` — id, name, manager_id
- `Goal` — id, title, description, level (company/team/individual), status (8 states), tag (daily→yearly), priority (critical/high/medium/low), weightage (auto from priority), start_date, due_date (auto from tag), creator_id, assignee_id, team_id, completion_percentage
- `Subtask` — id, goal_id, title, description, is_completed, completed_at
- `Progress` — id, goal_id, completion_percentage, notes, updated_at
- `Feedback` — id, goal_id, user_id, feedback_type (member/evaluator), ratings (quality/timeliness/innovation/collaboration/impact), deliverables, improvements
- `Score` — id, goal_id, rating (below/meets/above expectations), scored_by

**Goal lifecycle:**
```
DRAFT → PENDING_APPROVAL → ACTIVE → AWAITING_FEEDBACK → SCORABLE → SCORED
                                  ↘ COMPLETED
         ↘ REJECTED
```
(Admin/Manager assigning directly → skips to ACTIVE)

**Weightage system:** Priority maps to fixed % (critical=40, high=30, medium=20, low=10). Total per user per tag period cannot exceed 100%.

**API routes:**
- `POST /api/v1/auth/login`
- `GET/POST /api/v1/users/`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/v1/users/{id}`
- `GET/POST /api/v1/teams/`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/v1/teams/{id}`
- `GET/POST /api/v1/goals/`, `GET /api/v1/goals/{id}`
- `POST /api/v1/goals/{id}/submit|approve|progress|complete`
- `POST /api/v1/goals/{id}/subtasks`, `PATCH /api/v1/goals/subtasks/{id}`
- `POST /api/v1/goals/{id}/feedback/member|evaluator`
- `POST /api/v1/goals/{id}/score`
- `GET /api/v1/users/{user_id}/weightage/{tag}`

**Permissions:**
- Admin: full access
- Manager: approve/reject goals, submit evaluator feedback, score goals, manage own team
- Member: create/submit own goals, update progress, submit member feedback

---

### Frontend

**Tech stack:** React 18, Vite, Tailwind CSS, Zustand (auth store), React Hook Form, Axios, react-hot-toast, lucide-react, date-fns

**Pages:**
- `/login` — Login form
- `/` — Dashboard (role-aware: admin sees all, manager sees team, member sees own)
- `/goals/new` — Create goal (weightage check, auto due_date from tag)
- `/goals/:id` — Goal detail (full lifecycle actions, subtasks, feedback, score)
- `/users` — Admin: CRUD users
- `/teams` — Admin: CRUD teams

**Auth:** JWT stored in localStorage, Zustand store, axios interceptor auto-attaches Bearer token, 401 → redirect to login

---

### Key Business Rules
1. Weightage per user per tag period must sum ≤ 100%
2. Due date auto-calculated from start_date + tag duration
3. Both member AND evaluator feedback required before goal becomes SCORABLE
4. Only manager/admin of the assignee can approve goals
5. At-risk = time elapsed &gt; 70% AND completion &lt; 50%
6. Manager assigning goal to team member → auto-ACTIVE (no approval needed)

# PMS - High-Level Design (HLD)
## Performance &amp;amp; Goal Management Platform

**Document Version:** 1.0  
**Date:** March 2026  
**Status:** Architecture Blueprint

---

## 1. Executive Summary

The PMS Platform is a unified, enterprise-grade system designed to centralize:
- **Probation Management** — Automated Day 30/60/80 reviews with reminders &amp;amp; escalations
- **Performance Reviews** — Bi-annual and quarterly review cycles
- **Goal Management** — Structured goal creation, approval, and tracking with hierarchical cascading

**Key Differentiators:**
- Single source of truth for employee performance data
- Role-based access control (Employee, Manager, Admin)
- Automated workflow triggers and notifications
- Real-time dashboards for visibility and compliance

---

## 2. System Architecture Overview

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                          PRESENTATION LAYER                             │
│  ┌─────────────────┬──────────────────┬─────────────────┐              │
│  │  Employee UI    │   Manager UI     │    Admin UI     │              │
│  │  (React SPA)    │   (React SPA)    │   (React SPA)   │              │
│  └────────┬────────┴────────┬─────────┴────────┬────────┘              │
└───────────┼──────────────────┼──────────────────┼────────────────────────┘
            │                  │                  │
            └──────────────────┼──────────────────┘
                               │
┌──────────────────────────────▼────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                            API GATEWAY                                    │
│  - Request routing &amp;amp; authentication                                       │
│  - Rate limiting &amp;amp; API versioning                                         │
│  - Request/response transformation                                        │
└──────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                       │
        ┌──────────────┼──────────────┐
        │              │              │
┌───────▼────────┐ ┌──▼──────────┐ ┌─▼───────────────┐
│  GOAL SERVICE  │ │PROBATION S. │ │ REVIEW SERVICE  │
│                │ │             │ │                 │
│ • CRUD goals   │ │ • DOJ calc  │ │ • Cycles        │
│ • Approval WF  │ │ • Triggers  │ │ • Forms         │
│ • Weightage    │ │ • Reminders │ │ • Ratings       │
│ • Progress     │ │ • Escalate  │ │ • History       │
│ • Aggregation  │ │ • Status    │ │                 │
└────────┬───────┘ └──┬──────────┘ └─┬───────────────┘
         │            │              │
         └────────────┼──────────────┘
                      │
         ┌────────────┼────────────┐
         │            │            │
    ┌────▼──────┐ ┌──▼─────────┐ ┌─▼────────────┐
    │  AUTH SVC │ │ FEEDBACK S │ │NOTIFICATION │
    │           │ │            │ │   SERVICE   │
    │ • Login   │ │ • Store    │ │ • Email     │
    │ • Token   │ │ • Cross    │ │ • Reminders │
    │ • RBAC    │ │   Share    │ │ • Escalate  │
    └───────────┘ └────────────┘ └─────────────┘
                      │
         ┌────────────▼────────────┐
         │     SHARED DATABASE     │
         │  (PostgreSQL/MySQL)     │
         │                         │
         │ Tables:                 │
         │ • users                 │
         │ • goals                 │
         │ • goal_approvals        │
         │ • goal_progress         │
         │ • probation_records     │
         │ • probation_feedback    │
         │ • review_cycles         │
         │ • review_forms          │
         │ • feedback              │
         │ • notifications         │
         └─────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 3. Technology Stack

### Frontend

| Language | TypeScript | 5.9.3 |
| Build Tool | Vite | 8.0.1 |

### Backend
| Layer | Technology | Purpose |
|-------|-----------|---------|
| Runtime | Node.js | - |
| Framework | *(TBD: Express, NestJS, Fastify)* | API server |
| Database | PostgreSQL/MySQL | Persistent data |
| ORM | *(TBD: Prisma, TypeORM)* | Data access layer |
| Authentication | *(TBD: JWT, OAuth2)* | Auth &amp;amp; authorization |
| Job Queue | *(TBD: Bull, RabbitMQ)* | Scheduling &amp;amp; workflow |
| Cache | *(TBD: Redis)* | Performance optimization |

### DevOps
| Component | Technology |
|-----------|-----------|
| Containerization | Docker |
| Orchestration | Kubernetes / Docker Compose |
| CI/CD | GitHub Actions / Jenkins |
| Monitoring | *(TBD: Datadog, ELK)* |
| Logging | Centralized logging system |

---

## 4. Core Module Specifications

### 4.1 Goal Management Service (GMS)

**Responsibilities:**
- CRUD operations for goals
- Hierarchical goal structure (Company → Team → Individual)
- Goal approval workflow management
- Weightage validation (total must = 100%)
- Progress tracking and aggregation
- Goal versioning and history

**Key Entities:**
```
Goal
├── id
├── title
├── description
├── hierarchy_level (COMPANY, TEAM, INDIVIDUAL)
├── parent_id (for hierarchy)
├── weightage (0-100)
├── owner_id (Employee or Manager)
├── status (DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, ACTIVE, COMPLETED, ARCHIVED)
├── created_at
├── updated_at
└── created_by

GoalApproval
├── id
├── goal_id
├── approver_id (Manager or Admin)
├── approval_status (PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED)
├── feedback
├── approved_at

GoalProgress
├── id
├── goal_id
├── completion_percentage (0-100)
├── notes
├── updated_by
├── updated_at

GoalHistory
├── id
├── goal_id
├── change_type (CREATED, UPDATED, APPROVED, COMPLETED)
├── changed_by
├── changed_at
└── previous_values (JSON)
```

**API Endpoints:**
```
POST   /api/v1/goals                 # Create goal
GET    /api/v1/goals                 # List goals (with filters)
GET    /api/v1/goals/:id             # Get goal details
PUT    /api/v1/goals/:id             # Update goal
DELETE /api/v1/goals/:id             # Archive goal (soft delete)
POST   /api/v1/goals/:id/submit      # Submit for approval
POST   /api/v1/goals/:id/approve     # Approve goal
POST   /api/v1/goals/:id/reject      # Reject goal
PUT    /api/v1/goals/:id/progress    # Update progress %
GET    /api/v1/goals/stats/team      # Get team goal stats
GET    /api/v1/goals/stats/company   # Get company goal stats
```

**Workflow State Machine:**
```
DRAFT ──submit──&amp;gt; PENDING_APPROVAL ──approve──&amp;gt; ACTIVE ──complete──&amp;gt; COMPLETED ──archive──&amp;gt; ARCHIVED
                       │ reject                                            │ reopen
                       └──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### 4.2 Probation Service

**Responsibilities:**
- Track employee Date of Joining (DOJ)
- Calculate working days (excluding weekends &amp;amp; holidays)
- Trigger form generation at Day 30, 60, 80
- Manage reminder scheduling
- Handle escalation logic
- Store probation feedback
- Provide probation status tracking

**Key Entities:**
```
ProbationRecord
├── id
├── employee_id
├── date_of_joining
├── working_days_calculated
├── probation_status (IN_PROBATION, COMPLETED, REJECTED, PAUSED)
├── is_paused
├── pause_start_date
├── pause_resume_date
├── created_at

ProbationTrigger
├── id
├── probation_record_id
├── trigger_day (30, 60, 80)
├── trigger_date
├── status (PENDING, TRIGGERED, SUBMITTED, ESCALATED)
├── employee_form_id
├── manager_form_id
├── created_at

ProbationFeedback
├── id
├── probation_trigger_id
├── submitted_by_id (Employee or Manager)
├── feedback_type (SELF, MANAGER)
├── form_data (JSON)
├── submitted_at

ProbationReminder
├── id
├── probation_trigger_id
├── reminder_sent_at
├── reminder_count
├── next_reminder_at
```

**Scheduler Job:**
```
Every 6 hours:
  1. Get all employees in probation
  2. Calculate working days since DOJ
  3. For each trigger point (30, 60, 80):
     - If working days reached: create forms + send email
     - If triggered but not submitted:
       - +2 days: send reminder 1
       - +4 days: send reminder 2
       - +6 days: send reminder 3
       - +7 days: escalate to Admin
```

---

### 4.3 Review Service

**Responsibilities:**
- Create and manage review cycles
- Define cycle periods (Quarterly, Bi-annual)
- Generate review forms (self-assessment, manager feedback)
- Track form submissions
- Calculate final ratings
- Store performance history
- Generate compliance reports

**Key Entities:**
```
ReviewCycle
├── id
├── cycle_name (e.g., &amp;quot;Q1 2026&amp;quot;)
├── cycle_type (QUARTERLY, BI_ANNUAL, PROBATION)
├── start_date
├── end_date
├── self_review_deadline
├── manager_review_deadline
├── status (PENDING, ACTIVE, CLOSED)
├── created_by_id
├── created_at

ReviewForm
├── id
├── review_cycle_id
├── employee_id
├── manager_id
├── form_type (SELF_ASSESSMENT, MANAGER_FEEDBACK)
├── status (PENDING, IN_PROGRESS, SUBMITTED)
├── form_data (JSON)
├── final_rating (1-5 scale)
├── submitted_at
├── created_at

ReviewPerformanceHistory
├── id
├── employee_id
├── review_cycle_id
├── performance_score
├── rating
├── feedback_summary
├── created_at
```

**API Endpoints:**
```
POST   /api/v1/review-cycles        # Create cycle
GET    /api/v1/review-cycles        # List cycles
POST   /api/v1/review-cycles/:id/trigger  # Send cycle emails
GET    /api/v1/review-forms         # Get forms for user
POST   /api/v1/review-forms/:id/submit    # Submit form
GET    /api/v1/review-history       # Get performance history
```

---

### 4.4 Feedback Service

**Responsibilities:**
- Store feedback submissions
- Cross-share feedback between employee and manager
- Track feedback history
- Link feedback to goals
- Support versioning

**Key Entities:**
```
Feedback
├── id
├── source_id (whoever submitted)
├── target_id (whoever it&amp;#39;s about)
├── feedback_type (SELF, MANAGER, PEER)
├── related_to_id (goal_id or review_cycle_id)
├── content
├── rating (optional, 1-5)
├── is_shared
├── shared_at
├── created_at

FeedbackVersion
├── id
├── feedback_id
├── version_number
├── content
├── created_at
```

---

### 4.5 Authentication &amp;amp; Authorization Service

**Responsibilities:**
- User login/logout
- Token generation (JWT)
- Role-based access control (RBAC)
- Permission validation
- Session management

**Key Entities:**
```
User
├── id
├── email
├── first_name
├── last_name
├── password_hash
├── role (EMPLOYEE, MANAGER, ADMIN)
├── manager_id (nullable, links to direct manager)
├── department_id
├── date_of_joining
├── is_active
├── created_at
├── updated_at

RolePermission
├── id
├── role
├── permission
├── resource
└── action (CREATE, READ, UPDATE, DELETE)
```

**Role Permissions Matrix:**
```
ROLE: EMPLOYEE
├── Goals: Create own, Read own, Update own (draft), Submit own
├── Feedback: Submit self-feedback, Read received feedback
└── Reviews: Submit self-review, View history

ROLE: MANAGER
├── Goals: Create, Read team/own, Approve/Reject team
├── Feedback: Submit manager feedback, View team feedback
├── Reviews: Submit manager feedback for team, Set ratings
└── Escalations: View pending approvals, View alerts

ROLE: ADMIN
├── Goals: All operations on all goals
├── Feedback: View all feedback, Flag responses
├── Reviews: Trigger cycles, View compliance, Generate reports
├── Users: Manage user accounts, Assign roles
└── Reporting: Access all dashboards, Compliance views
```

---

### 4.6 Notification Service

**Responsibilities:**
- Send email notifications
- Schedule reminder notifications
- Track notification delivery
- Handle escalation alerts

**Notification Types:**
```
Probation Triggers
├── Day 30 email
├── Day 60 email
├── Day 80 email
└── Escalation notice

Goal Management
├── Goal submitted for approval (to Manager)
├── Goal approved/rejected (to Employee)
└── Ready for review (to Manager)

Performance Reviews
├── Cycle started (to all employees)
├── Self-review reminder (to Employee)
├── Manager feedback request (to Manager)
└── Cross-share notification (to both)

System Alerts
├── Escalation notice (to Admin)
├── Deadline approaching (to relevant users)
└── Compliance report (to Admin)
```

## 6. Database Schema (Normalized)

### Users &amp;amp; Auth
```sql
CREATE TABLE users (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  email VARCHAR UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  first_name VARCHAR,
  last_name VARCHAR,
  password_hash VARCHAR,
  role ENUM(EMPLOYEE, MANAGER, ADMIN),
  manager_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  department_id UUID,
  date_of_joining DATE,
  is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
  created_at TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE roles_permissions (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  role ENUM(EMPLOYEE, MANAGER, ADMIN),
  permission VARCHAR,
  resource VARCHAR,
  action VARCHAR
);
```

### Goals
```sql
CREATE TABLE goals (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  title VARCHAR NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  hierarchy_level ENUM(COMPANY, TEAM, INDIVIDUAL),
  parent_id UUID REFERENCES goals,
  owner_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  weightage DECIMAL(5,2),
  status ENUM(DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, ACTIVE, COMPLETED, ARCHIVED),
  created_by UUID REFERENCES users,
  created_at TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE goal_approvals (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  goal_id UUID REFERENCES goals,
  approver_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  approval_status ENUM(PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED),
  feedback TEXT,
  approved_at TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE goal_progress (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  goal_id UUID REFERENCES goals,
  completion_percentage INT,
  notes TEXT,
  updated_by UUID REFERENCES users,
  updated_at TIMESTAMP
);
```

### Probation
```sql
CREATE TABLE probation_records (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  employee_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  date_of_joining DATE,
  probation_status ENUM(IN_PROBATION, COMPLETED, REJECTED, PAUSED),
  is_paused BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  pause_start_date DATE,
  pause_resume_date DATE,
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE probation_triggers (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  probation_record_id UUID REFERENCES probation_records,
  trigger_day INT (30, 60, 80),
  trigger_date DATE,
  status ENUM(PENDING, TRIGGERED, SUBMITTED, ESCALATED),
  employee_form_id UUID,
  manager_form_id UUID,
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE probation_feedback (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  probation_trigger_id UUID REFERENCES probation_triggers,
  submitted_by_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  feedback_type ENUM(SELF, MANAGER),
  form_data JSONB,
  submitted_at TIMESTAMP
);
```

### Reviews
```sql
CREATE TABLE review_cycles (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  cycle_name VARCHAR,
  cycle_type ENUM(QUARTERLY, BI_ANNUAL, PROBATION),
  start_date DATE,
  end_date DATE,
  self_review_deadline DATE,
  manager_review_deadline DATE,
  status ENUM(PENDING, ACTIVE, CLOSED),
  created_by_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE review_forms (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  review_cycle_id UUID REFERENCES review_cycles,
  employee_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  manager_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  form_type ENUM(SELF_ASSESSMENT, MANAGER_FEEDBACK),
  status ENUM(PENDING, IN_PROGRESS, SUBMITTED),
  form_data JSONB,
  final_rating INT,
  submitted_at TIMESTAMP
);
```

### Feedback
```sql
CREATE TABLE feedback (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  source_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  target_id UUID REFERENCES users,
  feedback_type ENUM(SELF, MANAGER, PEER),
  related_to_id UUID,
  content TEXT,
  rating INT,
  is_shared BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  shared_at TIMESTAMP,
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);
```

---

## 7. API Design Principles

### Request/Response Format
```json
{
  &amp;quot;status&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;success|error&amp;quot;,
  &amp;quot;data&amp;quot;: { /* actual payload */ },
  &amp;quot;message&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;Human-readable message&amp;quot;,
  &amp;quot;timestamp&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;2026-03-27T10:30:00Z&amp;quot;,
  &amp;quot;errors&amp;quot;: [ /* validation errors */ ]
}
```

### Authentication
```
Header: Authorization: Bearer &amp;lt;JWT_TOKEN&amp;gt;
JWT Payload: { userId, role, permissions, exp }
```

### Pagination
```
Query: ?page=1&amp;amp;limit=20&amp;amp;sort=created_at&amp;amp;order=desc
Response: { data: [], pagination: { total, page, pages, limit } }
```

### Filtering &amp;amp; Search
```
Goals: ?status=ACTIVE&amp;amp;hierarchy_level=INDIVIDUAL&amp;amp;owner_id=xxx
Probation: ?status=IN_PROBATION&amp;amp;department_id=xxx
Reviews: ?cycle_type=QUARTERLY&amp;amp;year=2026
```

## 9. Key Workflows &amp;amp; State Machines

### 9.1 Goal Workflow
```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ DRAFT                                                   │
│ (Created by Employee or Manager)                        │
└──────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┘
               │ submit for approval
               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ PENDING_APPROVAL                                        │
│ (Awaiting Manager/Admin approval)                       │
└──┬──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┘
   │ reject                       │ approve
   │                              ▼
   │                  ┌──────────────────────────┐
   │                  │ ACTIVE                   │
   │                  │ (Can update progress)    │
   │                  └──────┬───────────────────┘
   │                         │ mark complete
   │                         ▼
   │                  ┌──────────────────────────┐
   │                  │ COMPLETED                │
   │                  │ (Cycle end or manual)    │
   │                  └──────┬───────────────────┘
   │                         │ archive
   │                         ▼
   │                  ┌──────────────────────────┐
   │                  │ ARCHIVED                 │
   │                  │ (Historical record)      │
   │                  └──────────────────────────┘
   │
   └──────────────► DRAFT (Edit &amp;amp; resubmit)
```

### 9.2 Probation Workflow
```
Employee Joins (DOJ tracked)
       │
       ▼ (Day 30 reached)
Forms Triggered
├── Employee self-feedback form
└── Manager feedback form
       │
       ▼ (User submits)
Submitted (awaiting peer)
       │
       ├─ If NOT submitted after 7 days: ESCALATE to Admin
       │        ├── Reminder 1: +2 days
       │        ├── Reminder 2: +4 days
       │        ├── Reminder 3: +6 days
       │        └── Escalate: +7 days
       │
       └─ Once both submitted: CROSS-SHARE feedback
       │
       ▼ (Repeat for Day 60 &amp;amp; Day 80)
Final Status:
├── On Day 85-90: Admin reviews &amp;amp; shares insights
├── Probation Confirmed: Employee cleared
└── Probation Rejected: Employee offboarding
```

### 9.3 Review Cycle Workflow
```
Admin Initiates Cycle (e.g., Q1 2026)
       │
       ▼
Email Trigger: Notify all in cycle
       │
       ▼
Self-Review Phase
├── Employees complete self-assessment
└── Deadline: specified date
       │
       ▼
Manager Review Phase
├── Managers review employee self-assessment
├── Provide manager feedback
└── Set final rating
       │
       ▼
Feedback Cross-Share
├── Both submitted: Share results
└── Pending: Send reminders
       │
       ▼
Close Cycle
└── Store performance history
```

---

## 10. Critical Integration Points

### 10.1 Notification Triggers
```
Probation Service  ──► Notification Service ──► Email Queue
Goal Service       ──► Notification Service ──► Email Queue
Review Service     ──► Notification Service ──► Email Queue
Escalation Rules   ──► Notification Service ──► Priority Queue
```

### 10.2 Data Flow Between Services
```
1. Goal Service ──► Review Service (link goals to reviews)
2. Probation Service ──► User Service (DOJ tracking)
3. Feedback Service ──► Goal/Review Service (link feedback)
4. Auth Service ──► All Services (RBAC verification)
```

### 10.3 Real-Time Updates
```
Frontend ─────► WebSocket ─────► Backend
                        ↓
            (Notifications, approvals, status updates)
```

---

## 11. Non-Functional Requirements

| Requirement | Target | Implementation |
|--|--|--|
| **Performance** | API response &amp;lt; 200ms | Caching, indexing, query optimization |
| **Availability** | 99.5% uptime | Load balancing, failover |
| **Security** | JWT auth + RBAC | SSL/TLS, password hashing, rate limiting |
| **Scalability** | Handle 10K concurrent users | Horizontal scaling, CDN, database sharding |
| **Data Backup** | Daily automated backups | Database replication, incremental backups |
| **Audit Trail** | Log all critical actions | Immutable audit logs, admin access logs |
| **Compliance** | GDPR-ready | Data anonymization, export capabilities |

---

## 12. Deployment Architecture

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                      CDN (CloudFront)                      │
└────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
             │
┌────────────▼────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    Load Balancer                            │
└────────────┬────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┘
             │                        │
┌────────────▼─────┐     ┌───────────▼──────┐
│  Backend Pod 1   │     │  Backend Pod 2   │
│  (API Service)   │     │  (API Service)   │
└────────┬─────────┘     └───────┬──────────┘
         │                       │
         └───────────┬───────────┘
                     │
          ┌──────────▼──────────┐
          │   PostgreSQL DB     │
          │ (Read Replicas)     │
          └─────────────────────┘
          
          ┌──────────────────────┐
          │   Redis Cache        │
          │ (Session + Data)     │
          └─────────────────────┘
          
          ┌──────────────────────┐
          │   Message Queue      │
          │ (Job Scheduler)      │
          └─────────────────────┘
```

## 14. Risk Mitigation

| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |
|--|--|--|
| Scope Creep | Schedule delay | Well-defined phases, strict PRD |
| Data Loss | Business continuity | Automated backups, disaster recovery plan |
| Performance Issues | User satisfaction | Load testing, query optimization early |
| Security Breach | Compliance + reputation | Security audit, penetration testing |
| Integration Complexity | Delivery delay | Clear API contracts, early integration testing |
| Resource Constraints | Quality issues | Proper staffing, knowledge sharing |

PMS Platform
Page 1
PMS
Performance &amp;amp; Goal Management Platform
Unified Product Requirements Document | March 2026
Roles in Scope: Employee | Manager | Admin (HR)
Modules: Performance Monitoring (PMS) | Goal Management System (GMS)
Review Cycles: Probation | Bi-Annual | Quarterly
1. Overview
PMS is an enterprise Performance &amp;amp; Goal Management Platform that unifies probation tracking, bi-
annual and quarterly performance reviews, and structured goal management into a single role-
based system.
The Goal Management System (GMS) is a core module within PMS — not a standalone product. It
shares the same role model, data layer, and interface as the broader platform.
PMS replaces fragmented email threads, spreadsheets, and informal feedback with automated
workflows, structured forms, and real-time dashboards. The platform serves three roles —
Employee, Manager, and Admin — each with a tailored experience.
2. Problem Statement
• No centralized visibility into probation timelines or review deadlines
• Feedback is informal, inconsistent, and not linked to goals
• Goal tracking is siloed across teams with no aggregation
• Admin has no real-time view into pending actions or red-flag responses
• Performance decisions lack structured, auditable data
• Goal creation is manager-only today, limiting employee ownership and accountability
3. Goals &amp;amp; Success Metrics
3.1 Platform Goals
• Automate all trigger emails for probation (Day 30/60/80) and review cycles
• Provide structured, role-appropriate feedback forms linked to GMS goal data
• Give Admin a live dashboard of submission status, flags, and trend analysis
• Cascade goals from Company → Team → Individual with weighted completion tracking
• Enable employees to create and propose goals, subject to manager or admin approval
PMS Platform
Page 2
3.2 Success Metrics
Metric Target
Probation triggers sent on schedule 100% with zero manual intervention
Feedback form completion rate &amp;gt; 85% before escalation threshold
Admin flag review turnaround &amp;lt; 7 days
Manager goal assignment coverage &amp;gt; 90% within first 30 days of cycle
Employee goal approval turnaround &amp;lt; 5 business days
4. Users &amp;amp; Roles
Three roles are in scope: Employee, Manager, and Admin. Admin subsumes all HR and
leadership/CXO access — there is no fourth role.
Feature Employee Manager Admin
Dashboard Personal goals +
feedback history + self-

5. Core Modules
PMS Platform
Page 3
5.1 Probation Monitoring
Automated email triggers go out from the employee&amp;#39;s date of joining (DOJ) at Day 30, Day 60, and
Day 80. Each trigger sends one form to the employee (self-feedback) and one to their manager.
Submissions are stored and cross-shared between employee and manager upon completion.
• Day 30 — initial check-in form
• Day 60 — mid-probation form
• Day 80 — final form, 10 days before confirmation discussion
• Reminder at +2 days if not submitted; escalation to Admin after 7 days
• Day 85–90 — Admin reviews all feedback and shares insights with manager before
confirmation call
Edge Case Handling — Probation
• Leave during probation: probation clock pauses on leave, resumes on return. Admin
dashboard shows &amp;#39;Paused&amp;#39; badge with revised expected trigger date.
• Manager change mid-probation: pending forms reassigned to new manager; prior
submissions visible in read-only probation history with context note.
• Early termination: all pending triggers auto-cancel with audit log entry.
• Cross-share logic: forms only cross-shared after both parties submit. Status shown as
&amp;#39;Waiting for [Manager/Employee]&amp;#39; until then.
• No manager assigned: trigger blocked, Admin alerted with one-click resolution.
• Backdated DOJ: system prompts Admin to send missed forms or mark as waived; waiver
logged for audit. Never auto-fires without confirmation.
5.2 Performance Review Cycles
Two parallel review tracks run for different employee segments.
Bi-Annual (majority of employees)
• Cycle 1: April–September goals — trigger Aug 1, close Aug 25, finalize from Aug 26
• Cycle 2: October–March goals — trigger Feb 1, close Feb 25, finalize from Feb 26
Quarterly (select roles / leadership)
• Q1 (Jan–Mar): Trigger Apr 1, close Apr 15
• Q2 (Apr–Jun): Trigger Jul 1, close Jul 15
• Q3 (Jul–Sep): Trigger Oct 1, close Oct 15
• Q4 (Oct–Dec): Trigger Jan 1, close Jan 15
Reminder schedule for all cycles: 5th (gentle nudge), 15th (urgent), 22nd (escalation to Admin if
pending).
PMS Platform
Page 4
Edge Case Handling — Review Cycles
• Mid-cycle joiners: eligibility cutoff = joined more than 60 days before cycle close. Otherwise
auto-enrolled in next cycle. Shown clearly on employee profile.
• Dual-track employees (quarterly + bi-annual): system deduplicates. If quarterly window
overlaps bi-annual, run quarterly only and log the skip.
• Manager unavailable during finalization: Admin can designate a temporary acting reviewer
with audit trail.
• Cycle close with unsubmitted forms: Admin gets list with options — extend window,
escalate, or mark as waived. All decisions logged.
5.3 Goal Management System (GMS)
GMS is the goal tracking module within PMS. It is not a standalone product. All goal data, user
roles, and feedback are shared with the broader PMS platform.
Goal Hierarchy
• Goals cascade: Company → Team → Individual
• Weightage-based completion % auto-aggregates to team and company level
• Performance rating: Below / Meets / Above Expectations
Goal Lifecycle &amp;amp; Approval Flow
Employees can create their own goals. All employee-created goals require approval before
becoming active.
Status Description
Draft Created by employee, not yet submitted
Pending Approval Submitted by employee, awaiting manager or admin action

Approval Flow Details
• On submission: manager notified immediately
• On approval: employee notified, goal status set to Active, manager sets weightage
• On rejection: employee notified with reason/comment, can edit and resubmit
• Admin can approve any goal, including if manager is unavailable
• Approval turnaround target: 5 business days
Edge Case Handling — GMS
• No goals at review time: self-rating blocked. Employee sees message to contact manager.
Manager alerted simultaneously.
• Deleted/archived goals: retained in historical record, frozen contribution to completion % for
the cycle in which they existed.
• Weightage validation: real-time counter shown during goal assignment. Turns red if total is
not 100%. Save blocked until balanced.
• Goal ownership after team transfer: goals follow employee. Old manager retains view
access for current cycle; new manager gets edit access.
• Company goals updated mid-cycle: changes propagate as suggestions, not forced
overwrites. Team/individual owners get 5 days to acknowledge before Admin is notified.
5.4 Feedback &amp;amp; Flag Module
All form submissions (probation and cycle reviews) feed into a single unified response store. The
system auto-tags red flags based on low scores or negative open-ended responses. Admin reviews
flagged entries weekly.
• Cross-share: manager sees employee self-feedback; employee sees manager feedback —
only after both parties submit
• Red flag tagging on scores below configured threshold or negative sentiment
• Admin weekly review queue with action tracking
• All feedback linked to GMS goal data for context
• Blank open-ended responses treated as soft flags — surfaced to Admin as &amp;#39;incomplete&amp;#39;
PMS Platform
Page 6
Edge Case Handling — Feedback &amp;amp; Flags
• Unconfigured red-flag threshold: mandatory setup step in onboarding checklist. Platform will
not activate review cycles until configured. Default recommendation: score &amp;lt;= 2 out of 5.
• Flags with no Admin action in 7 days: auto-escalate to secondary Admin contact. Flag aging
indicator shown in dashboard.
• Repeat flags across cycles: pattern detection layer surfaces &amp;#39;Repeat Flag&amp;#39; alert if flagged in
2+ consecutive cycles. All flagged submissions shown side by side.

It looks like you have shared a comprehensive Product Requirements Document (PRD) or Functional Specification for a Performance Management System (PMS) with an integrated Goal Management System (GMS).

This is a well-structured document with a strong emphasis on automation, accountability, and handling edge cases (like paused probation clocks and mid-cycle joiners).

Here is a quick high-level summary of the system you&amp;#39;ve outlined:

Automated Review Workflows: System-driven triggers for probation (30/60/80 working days) and standard review cycles (Bi-Annual and Quarterly) with built-in reminder and escalation paths.

Goal Management System (GMS): A cascading goal structure (Company → Team → Individual) where employees can propose goals that go through a manager/admin approval workflow before becoming active and weighted.

Role-Based Access: A clean three-tier structure (Employee, Manager, Admin) where Admins act as the ultimate fail-safe for overrides, reassignments, and escalations.

Feedback &amp;amp; Risk Mitigation: A unified feedback module that cross-shares responses only when both parties complete them, equipped with an automated red-flag system for low scores or negative sentiment.

Clear Prioritization: A phased approach where core CRUD, basic workflows, and automation are P0, while advanced analytics, pattern detection, and reporting are P2.

How would you like to use this information? I can help you build upon this specification. Would you like me to:

Analyze for logical gaps or identify missing edge cases in the approval workflows?

Generate a QA testing checklist based on the automation rules and edge-case handling?

Gemini said
This PRD lays a rock-solid foundation for a robust Performance Management System. To elevate this from a standard CRUD application to a truly &amp;quot;Pro,&amp;quot; enterprise-grade platform, we need to focus on use cases that drive massive operational efficiency for HR (Admins) and eliminate friction for Employees and Managers.

Here are the most impactful, high-value use cases we can deliver based on your specifications, broken down by the core value they provide.

1. The &amp;quot;Zero-Slip&amp;quot; Automated Probation Engine
The Impact: Probation tracking is often highly manual and prone to HR dropping the ball. If an employee misses their confirmation window, it creates legal and compliance risks.
The Pro Use Case: We deliver a completely autonomous, resilient background engine.

Dynamic Timers: The system doesn&amp;#39;t just count calendar days; it actively calculates working days and automatically pauses/resumes the 30/60/80-day clocks when an employee logs a leave of absence.

Fail-Safe Escalation Routing: If a manager leaves the company mid-probation, the system detects the orphaned employee and immediately pauses the trigger, firing a high-priority alert to the Admin queue for reassignment before a deadline is missed.

Audit-Proof Logging: Every automated email, reminder nudge, and Admin waiver is logged with a timestamp, creating an immutable compliance trail.

2. The Real-Time Admin &amp;quot;Command Center&amp;quot;
The Impact: Admins (HR/Leadership) usually have to dig through spreadsheets to find out who hasn&amp;#39;t completed their reviews.
The Pro Use Case: Instead of a simple list view, we build a high-visibility, real-time analytics dashboard that provides immediate operational intelligence.

Aggregated Risk Metrics: The dashboard surfaces organizational bottlenecks—for example, automatically highlighting if a specific department (e.g., &amp;quot;Engineering&amp;quot;) has a 40% overdue rate for goal approvals.

The &amp;quot;Red-Flag&amp;quot; Triage Queue: As feedback forms are submitted, the sentiment analysis and low-score triggers instantly populate a centralized inbox for the Admin. They can see the flagged response, the historical context of that employee, and the goal performance side-by-side to make immediate intervention decisions.

3. Dynamic Goal Cascading &amp;amp; Weightage Integrity
The Impact: In many systems, when a company goal changes mid-year, individual goals break, or weightages push past 100%, causing chaos during final performance ratings.
The Pro Use Case: We build a strictly validated, top-down goal architecture.

Real-Time Weight Validation: As a manager assigns goals, the UI enforces strict validation. If the weightage hits 105%, the &amp;quot;Save/Approve&amp;quot; state is locked, preventing bad data from ever entering the database.

Smart Propagation: If the Admin updates a top-level Company Goal mid-cycle, the system doesn&amp;#39;t force-overwrite team data. Instead, it triggers a &amp;quot;Review Required&amp;quot; state for associated Team Goals, giving managers a 5-day SLA to adjust their cascading metrics before escalating to HR.

4. The &amp;quot;Blind&amp;quot; Cross-Share Feedback Mechanism
The Impact: Bias is a massive problem in performance reviews. If an employee sees a manager&amp;#39;s harsh review before writing their self-review, they will alter their responses (and vice versa).
The Pro Use Case: We implement a strict dual-lock system for cycle feedback.

Asynchronous Submissions: The employee and manager fill out their forms independently. The system displays a &amp;quot;Waiting for Manager&amp;quot; or &amp;quot;Waiting for Employee&amp;quot; state.

The Reveal: The payload is only unlocked and cross-shared to both dashboards the exact second the final party hits &amp;quot;Submit.&amp;quot; This ensures completely unvarnished, authentic feedback from both sides, which is crucial for fair evaluations.

How to Execute This
To build this effectively, the backend architecture will need to rely heavily on robust job scheduling (for the precise daily triggers) and a highly optimized relational database structure to handle the recursive nature of the Company → Team → Individual goal cascading without performance lag.

Would you like me to map out the specific backend API endpoints required to support the P0 features, or should we draft the detailed user stories for the Admin Command Center first?

# PMS - High-Level Design (HLD)

## Performance &amp;amp; Goal Management Platform



**Document Version:** 1.0  

**Date:** March 2026  

**Status:** Architecture Blueprint



---



## 1. Executive Summary



The PMS Platform is a unified, enterprise-grade system designed to centralize:

- **Probation Management** — Automated Day 30/60/80 reviews with reminders &amp;amp; escalations

- **Performance Reviews** — Bi-annual and quarterly review cycles

- **Goal Management** — Structured goal creation, approval, and tracking with hierarchical cascading



**Key Differentiators:**

- Single source of truth for employee performance data

- Role-based access control (Employee, Manager, Admin)

- Automated workflow triggers and notifications

- Real-time dashboards for visibility and compliance



---



## 2. System Architecture Overview



```

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

│                          PRESENTATION LAYER                             │

│  ┌─────────────────┬──────────────────┬─────────────────┐              │

│  │  Employee UI    │   Manager UI     │    Admin UI     │              │

│  │  (React SPA)    │   (React SPA)    │   (React SPA)   │              │

│  └────────┬────────┴────────┬─────────┴────────┬────────┘              │

└───────────┼──────────────────┼──────────────────┼────────────────────────┘

            │                  │                  │

            └──────────────────┼──────────────────┘

                               │

┌──────────────────────────────▼────────────────────────────────────────────┐

│                            API GATEWAY                                    │

│  - Request routing &amp;amp; authentication                                       │

│  - Rate limiting &amp;amp; API versioning                                         │

│  - Request/response transformation                                        │

└──────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

                       │

        ┌──────────────┼──────────────┐

        │              │              │

┌───────▼────────┐ ┌──▼──────────┐ ┌─▼───────────────┐

│  GOAL SERVICE  │ │PROBATION S. │ │ REVIEW SERVICE  │

│                │ │             │ │                 │

│ • CRUD goals   │ │ • DOJ calc  │ │ • Cycles        │

│ • Approval WF  │ │ • Triggers  │ │ • Forms         │

│ • Weightage    │ │ • Reminders │ │ • Ratings       │

│ • Progress     │ │ • Escalate  │ │ • History       │

│ • Aggregation  │ │ • Status    │ │                 │

└────────┬───────┘ └──┬──────────┘ └─┬───────────────┘

         │            │              │

         └────────────┼──────────────┘

                      │

         ┌────────────┼────────────┐

         │            │            │

    ┌────▼──────┐ ┌──▼─────────┐ ┌─▼────────────┐

    │  AUTH SVC │ │ FEEDBACK S │ │NOTIFICATION │

    │           │ │            │ │   SERVICE   │

    │ • Login   │ │ • Store    │ │ • Email     │

    │ • Token   │ │ • Cross    │ │ • Reminders │

    │ • RBAC    │ │   Share    │ │ • Escalate  │

    └───────────┘ └────────────┘ └─────────────┘

                      │

         ┌────────────▼────────────┐

         │     SHARED DATABASE     │

         │  (PostgreSQL/MySQL)     │

         │                         │

         │ Tables:                 │

         │ • users                 │

         │ • goals                 │

         │ • goal_approvals        │

         │ • goal_progress         │

         │ • probation_records     │

         │ • probation_feedback    │

         │ • review_cycles         │

         │ • review_forms          │

         │ • feedback              │

         │ • notifications         │

         └─────────────────────────┘

```

## 4. Core Module Specifications



### 4.1 Goal Management Service (GMS)



**Responsibilities:**

- CRUD operations for goals

- Hierarchical goal structure (Company → Team → Individual)

- Goal approval workflow management

- Weightage validation (total must = 100%)

- Progress tracking and aggregation

- Goal versioning and history



**Key Entities:**

```

Goal

├── id

├── title

├── description

├── hierarchy_level (COMPANY, TEAM, INDIVIDUAL)

├── parent_id (for hierarchy)

├── weightage (0-100)

├── owner_id (Employee or Manager)

├── status (DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, ACTIVE, COMPLETED, ARCHIVED)

├── created_at

├── updated_at

└── created_by



GoalApproval

├── id

├── goal_id

├── approver_id (Manager or Admin)

├── approval_status (PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED)

├── feedback

├── approved_at



GoalProgress

├── id

├── goal_id

├── completion_percentage (0-100)

├── notes

├── updated_by

├── updated_at



GoalHistory

├── id

├── goal_id

├── change_type (CREATED, UPDATED, APPROVED, COMPLETED)

├── changed_by

├── changed_at

└── previous_values (JSON)

```



**API Endpoints:**

```

POST   /api/v1/goals                 # Create goal

GET    /api/v1/goals                 # List goals (with filters)

GET    /api/v1/goals/:id             # Get goal details

PUT    /api/v1/goals/:id             # Update goal

DELETE /api/v1/goals/:id             # Archive goal (soft delete)

POST   /api/v1/goals/:id/submit      # Submit for approval

POST   /api/v1/goals/:id/approve     # Approve goal

POST   /api/v1/goals/:id/reject      # Reject goal

PUT    /api/v1/goals/:id/progress    # Update progress %

GET    /api/v1/goals/stats/team      # Get team goal stats

GET    /api/v1/goals/stats/company   # Get company goal stats

```



**Workflow State Machine:**

```

DRAFT ──submit──&amp;gt; PENDING_APPROVAL ──approve──&amp;gt; ACTIVE ──complete──&amp;gt; COMPLETED ──archive──&amp;gt; ARCHIVED

                       │ reject                                            │ reopen

                       └──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



---



### 4.2 Probation Service



**Responsibilities:**

- Track employee Date of Joining (DOJ)

- Calculate working days (excluding weekends &amp;amp; holidays)

- Trigger form generation at Day 30, 60, 80

- Manage reminder scheduling

- Handle escalation logic

- Store probation feedback

- Provide probation status tracking



**Key Entities:**

```

ProbationRecord

├── id

├── employee_id

├── date_of_joining

├── working_days_calculated

├── probation_status (IN_PROBATION, COMPLETED, REJECTED, PAUSED)

├── is_paused

├── pause_start_date

├── pause_resume_date

├── created_at



ProbationTrigger

├── id

├── probation_record_id

├── trigger_day (30, 60, 80)

├── trigger_date

├── status (PENDING, TRIGGERED, SUBMITTED, ESCALATED)

├── employee_form_id

├── manager_form_id

├── created_at



ProbationFeedback

├── id

├── probation_trigger_id

├── submitted_by_id (Employee or Manager)

├── feedback_type (SELF, MANAGER)

├── form_data (JSON)

├── submitted_at



ProbationReminder

├── id

├── probation_trigger_id

├── reminder_sent_at

├── reminder_count

├── next_reminder_at

```



**Scheduler Job:**

```

Every 6 hours:

  1. Get all employees in probation

  2. Calculate working days since DOJ

  3. For each trigger point (30, 60, 80):

     - If working days reached: create forms + send email

     - If triggered but not submitted:

       - +2 days: send reminder 1

       - +4 days: send reminder 2

       - +6 days: send reminder 3

       - +7 days: escalate to Admin

```



---



### 4.3 Review Service



**Responsibilities:**

- Create and manage review cycles

- Define cycle periods (Quarterly, Bi-annual)

- Generate review forms (self-assessment, manager feedback)

- Track form submissions

- Calculate final ratings

- Store performance history

- Generate compliance reports



**Key Entities:**

```

ReviewCycle

├── id

├── cycle_name (e.g., &amp;quot;Q1 2026&amp;quot;)

├── cycle_type (QUARTERLY, BI_ANNUAL, PROBATION)

├── start_date

├── end_date

├── self_review_deadline

├── manager_review_deadline

├── status (PENDING, ACTIVE, CLOSED)

├── created_by_id

├── created_at



ReviewForm

├── id

├── review_cycle_id

├── employee_id

├── manager_id

├── form_type (SELF_ASSESSMENT, MANAGER_FEEDBACK)

├── status (PENDING, IN_PROGRESS, SUBMITTED)

├── form_data (JSON)

├── final_rating (1-5 scale)

├── submitted_at

├── created_at



ReviewPerformanceHistory

├── id

├── employee_id

├── review_cycle_id

├── performance_score

├── rating

├── feedback_summary

├── created_at

```



**API Endpoints:**

```

POST   /api/v1/review-cycles        # Create cycle

GET    /api/v1/review-cycles        # List cycles

POST   /api/v1/review-cycles/:id/trigger  # Send cycle emails

GET    /api/v1/review-forms         # Get forms for user

POST   /api/v1/review-forms/:id/submit    # Submit form

GET    /api/v1/review-history       # Get performance history

```



---



### 4.4 Feedback Service



**Responsibilities:**

- Store feedback submissions

- Cross-share feedback between employee and manager

- Track feedback history

- Link feedback to goals

- Support versioning



**Key Entities:**

```

Feedback

├── id

├── source_id (whoever submitted)

├── target_id (whoever it&amp;#39;s about)

├── feedback_type (SELF, MANAGER, PEER)

├── related_to_id (goal_id or review_cycle_id)

├── content

├── rating (optional, 1-5)

├── is_shared

├── shared_at

├── created_at



FeedbackVersion

├── id

├── feedback_id

├── version_number

├── content

├── created_at

```



---



### 4.5 Authentication &amp;amp; Authorization Service



**Responsibilities:**

- User login/logout

- Token generation (JWT)

- Role-based access control (RBAC)

- Permission validation

- Session management



**Key Entities:**

```

User

├── id

├── email

├── first_name

├── last_name

├── password_hash

├── role (EMPLOYEE, MANAGER, ADMIN)

├── manager_id (nullable, links to direct manager)

├── department_id

├── date_of_joining

├── is_active

├── created_at

├── updated_at



RolePermission

├── id

├── role

├── permission

├── resource

└── action (CREATE, READ, UPDATE, DELETE)

```



**Role Permissions Matrix:**

```

ROLE: EMPLOYEE

├── Goals: Create own, Read own, Update own (draft), Submit own

├── Feedback: Submit self-feedback, Read received feedback

└── Reviews: Submit self-review, View history



ROLE: MANAGER

├── Goals: Create, Read team/own, Approve/Reject team

├── Feedback: Submit manager feedback, View team feedback

├── Reviews: Submit manager feedback for team, Set ratings

└── Escalations: View pending approvals, View alerts



ROLE: ADMIN

├── Goals: All operations on all goals

├── Feedback: View all feedback, Flag responses

├── Reviews: Trigger cycles, View compliance, Generate reports

├── Users: Manage user accounts, Assign roles

└── Reporting: Access all dashboards, Compliance views

```



---



### 4.6 Notification Service



**Responsibilities:**

- Send email notifications

- Schedule reminder notifications

- Track notification delivery

- Handle escalation alerts



**Notification Types:**

```

Probation Triggers

├── Day 30 email

├── Day 60 email

├── Day 80 email

└── Escalation notice



Goal Management

├── Goal submitted for approval (to Manager)

├── Goal approved/rejected (to Employee)

└── Ready for review (to Manager)



Performance Reviews

├── Cycle started (to all employees)

├── Self-review reminder (to Employee)

├── Manager feedback request (to Manager)

└── Cross-share notification (to both)



System Alerts

├── Escalation notice (to Admin)

├── Deadline approaching (to relevant users)

└── Compliance report (to Admin)

```

## 6. Database Schema (Normalized)



### Users &amp;amp; Auth

```sql

CREATE TABLE users (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  email VARCHAR UNIQUE NOT NULL,

  first_name VARCHAR,

  last_name VARCHAR,

  password_hash VARCHAR,

  role ENUM(EMPLOYEE, MANAGER, ADMIN),

  manager_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  department_id UUID,

  date_of_joining DATE,

  is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,

  created_at TIMESTAMP,

  updated_at TIMESTAMP

);



CREATE TABLE roles_permissions (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  role ENUM(EMPLOYEE, MANAGER, ADMIN),

  permission VARCHAR,

  resource VARCHAR,

  action VARCHAR

);

```



### Goals

```sql

CREATE TABLE goals (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  title VARCHAR NOT NULL,

  description TEXT,

  hierarchy_level ENUM(COMPANY, TEAM, INDIVIDUAL),

  parent_id UUID REFERENCES goals,

  owner_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  weightage DECIMAL(5,2),

  status ENUM(DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, ACTIVE, COMPLETED, ARCHIVED),

  created_by UUID REFERENCES users,

  created_at TIMESTAMP,

  updated_at TIMESTAMP

);



CREATE TABLE goal_approvals (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  goal_id UUID REFERENCES goals,

  approver_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  approval_status ENUM(PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED),

  feedback TEXT,

  approved_at TIMESTAMP

);



CREATE TABLE goal_progress (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  goal_id UUID REFERENCES goals,

  completion_percentage INT,

  notes TEXT,

  updated_by UUID REFERENCES users,

  updated_at TIMESTAMP

);

```



### Probation

```sql

CREATE TABLE probation_records (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  employee_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  date_of_joining DATE,

  probation_status ENUM(IN_PROBATION, COMPLETED, REJECTED, PAUSED),

  is_paused BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,

  pause_start_date DATE,

  pause_resume_date DATE,

  created_at TIMESTAMP

);



CREATE TABLE probation_triggers (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  probation_record_id UUID REFERENCES probation_records,

  trigger_day INT (30, 60, 80),

  trigger_date DATE,

  status ENUM(PENDING, TRIGGERED, SUBMITTED, ESCALATED),

  employee_form_id UUID,

  manager_form_id UUID,

  created_at TIMESTAMP

);



CREATE TABLE probation_feedback (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  probation_trigger_id UUID REFERENCES probation_triggers,

  submitted_by_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  feedback_type ENUM(SELF, MANAGER),

  form_data JSONB,

  submitted_at TIMESTAMP

);

```



### Reviews

```sql

CREATE TABLE review_cycles (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  cycle_name VARCHAR,

  cycle_type ENUM(QUARTERLY, BI_ANNUAL, PROBATION),

  start_date DATE,

  end_date DATE,

  self_review_deadline DATE,

  manager_review_deadline DATE,

  status ENUM(PENDING, ACTIVE, CLOSED),

  created_by_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  created_at TIMESTAMP

);



CREATE TABLE review_forms (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  review_cycle_id UUID REFERENCES review_cycles,

  employee_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  manager_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  form_type ENUM(SELF_ASSESSMENT, MANAGER_FEEDBACK),

  status ENUM(PENDING, IN_PROGRESS, SUBMITTED),

  form_data JSONB,

  final_rating INT,

  submitted_at TIMESTAMP

);

```



### Feedback

```sql

CREATE TABLE feedback (

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

  source_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  target_id UUID REFERENCES users,

  feedback_type ENUM(SELF, MANAGER, PEER),

  related_to_id UUID,

  content TEXT,

  rating INT,

  is_shared BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,

  shared_at TIMESTAMP,

  created_at TIMESTAMP

);

```



---



## 7. API Design Principles



### Request/Response Format

```json

{

  &amp;quot;status&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;success|error&amp;quot;,

  &amp;quot;data&amp;quot;: { /* actual payload */ },

  &amp;quot;message&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;Human-readable message&amp;quot;,

  &amp;quot;timestamp&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;2026-03-27T10:30:00Z&amp;quot;,

  &amp;quot;errors&amp;quot;: [ /* validation errors */ ]

}

```



### Authentication

```

Header: Authorization: Bearer &amp;lt;JWT_TOKEN&amp;gt;

JWT Payload: { userId, role, permissions, exp }

```



### Pagination

```

Query: ?page=1&amp;amp;limit=20&amp;amp;sort=created_at&amp;amp;order=desc

Response: { data: [], pagination: { total, page, pages, limit } }

```



### Filtering &amp;amp; Search

```

Goals: ?status=ACTIVE&amp;amp;hierarchy_level=INDIVIDUAL&amp;amp;owner_id=xxx

Probation: ?status=IN_PROBATION&amp;amp;department_id=xxx

Reviews: ?cycle_type=QUARTERLY&amp;amp;year=2026

```

## 9. Key Workflows &amp;amp; State Machines



### 9.1 Goal Workflow

```

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

│ DRAFT                                                   │

│ (Created by Employee or Manager)                        │

└──────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┘

               │ submit for approval

               ▼

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

│ PENDING_APPROVAL                                        │

│ (Awaiting Manager/Admin approval)                       │

└──┬──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┘

   │ reject                       │ approve

   │                              ▼

   │                  ┌──────────────────────────┐

   │                  │ ACTIVE                   │

   │                  │ (Can update progress)    │

   │                  └──────┬───────────────────┘

   │                         │ mark complete

   │                         ▼

   │                  ┌──────────────────────────┐

   │                  │ COMPLETED                │

   │                  │ (Cycle end or manual)    │

   │                  └──────┬───────────────────┘

   │                         │ archive

   │                         ▼

   │                  ┌──────────────────────────┐

   │                  │ ARCHIVED                 │

   │                  │ (Historical record)      │

   │                  └──────────────────────────┘

   │

   └──────────────► DRAFT (Edit &amp;amp; resubmit)

```



### 9.2 Probation Workflow

```

Employee Joins (DOJ tracked)

       │

       ▼ (Day 30 reached)

Forms Triggered

├── Employee self-feedback form

└── Manager feedback form

       │

       ▼ (User submits)

Submitted (awaiting peer)

       │

       ├─ If NOT submitted after 7 days: ESCALATE to Admin

       │        ├── Reminder 1: +2 days

       │        ├── Reminder 2: +4 days

       │        ├── Reminder 3: +6 days

       │        └── Escalate: +7 days

       │

       └─ Once both submitted: CROSS-SHARE feedback

       │

       ▼ (Repeat for Day 60 &amp;amp; Day 80)

Final Status:

├── On Day 85-90: Admin reviews &amp;amp; shares insights

├── Probation Confirmed: Employee cleared

└── Probation Rejected: Employee offboarding

```



### 9.3 Review Cycle Workflow

```

Admin Initiates Cycle (e.g., Q1 2026)

       │

       ▼

Email Trigger: Notify all in cycle

       │

       ▼

Self-Review Phase

├── Employees complete self-assessment

└── Deadline: specified date

       │

       ▼

Manager Review Phase

├── Managers review employee self-assessment

├── Provide manager feedback

└── Set final rating

       │

       ▼

Feedback Cross-Share

├── Both submitted: Share results

└── Pending: Send reminders

       │

       ▼

Close Cycle

└── Store performance history

```



---



## 10. Critical Integration Points



### 10.1 Notification Triggers

```

Probation Service  ──► Notification Service ──► Email Queue

Goal Service       ──► Notification Service ──► Email Queue

Review Service     ──► Notification Service ──► Email Queue

Escalation Rules   ──► Notification Service ──► Priority Queue

```



### 10.2 Data Flow Between Services

```

1. Goal Service ──► Review Service (link goals to reviews)

2. Probation Service ──► User Service (DOJ tracking)

3. Feedback Service ──► Goal/Review Service (link feedback)

4. Auth Service ──► All Services (RBAC verification)

```



### 10.3 Real-Time Updates

```

Frontend ─────► WebSocket ─────► Backend

                        ↓

            (Notifications, approvals, status updates)

```



---



## 11. Non-Functional Requirements



| Requirement | Target | Implementation |

|--|--|--|

| **Performance** | API response &amp;lt; 200ms | Caching, indexing, query optimization |

| **Availability** | 99.5% uptime | Load balancing, failover |

| **Security** | JWT auth + RBAC | SSL/TLS, password hashing, rate limiting |

| **Scalability** | Handle 10K concurrent users | Horizontal scaling, CDN, database sharding |

| **Data Backup** | Daily automated backups | Database replication, incremental backups |

| **Audit Trail** | Log all critical actions | Immutable audit logs, admin access logs |

| **Compliance** | GDPR-ready | Data anonymization, export capabilities |



---



## 12. Deployment Architecture



```

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

│                      CDN (CloudFront)                      │

└────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

             │

┌────────────▼────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

│                    Load Balancer                            │

└────────────┬────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┘

             │                        │

┌────────────▼─────┐     ┌───────────▼──────┐

│  Backend Pod 1   │     │  Backend Pod 2   │

│  (API Service)   │     │  (API Service)   │

└────────┬─────────┘     └───────┬──────────┘

         │                       │

         └───────────┬───────────┘

                     │

          ┌──────────▼──────────┐

          │   PostgreSQL DB     │

          │ (Read Replicas)     │

          └─────────────────────┘

          

          ┌──────────────────────┐

          │   Redis Cache        │

          │ (Session + Data)     │

          └─────────────────────┘

          

          ┌──────────────────────┐

          │   Message Queue      │

          │ (Job Scheduler)      │

          └─────────────────────┘

```

## 14. Risk Mitigation



| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |

|--|--|--|

| Scope Creep | Schedule delay | Well-defined phases, strict PRD |

| Data Loss | Business continuity | Automated backups, disaster recovery plan |

| Performance Issues | User satisfaction | Load testing, query optimization early |

| Security Breach | Compliance + reputation | Security audit, penetration testing |

| Integration Complexity | Delivery delay | Clear API contracts, early integration testing |

| Resource Constraints | Quality issues | Proper staffing, knowledge sharing |

- **API response time:** 95th percentile &amp;lt; 200ms

- **User adoption:** &amp;gt; 80% active monthly users within 3 months

Gemini said
This High-Level Design (HLD) is exceptionally well-architected. You have laid out a highly normalized database schema, clear domain boundaries for your microservices, and a robust state-machine for the review and goal workflows.

To elevate this platform from a standard HR tool to a &amp;quot;Pro,&amp;quot; enterprise-grade system, we need to look beyond basic CRUD operations and focus on use cases that leverage the relational data to generate proactive business intelligence.

Here are the most impactful, high-value use cases we can deliver on top of this architecture, optimized for enterprise scale.

1. Real-Time Org Health &amp;amp; Flight Risk Detection
The Problem: Traditional systems only tell HR that an employee is failing after the review cycle closes, which is often too late to prevent turnover.
The Pro Use Case: We utilize the continuous data streams from the Goal Service and Probation Service to build a predictive analytics engine.

How it works: By tracking the delta in goal progress updates (e.g., an employee who usually updates goals weekly suddenly stops for 40 days) combined with sentiment analysis on Day 30/60 check-ins, the system can auto-flag &amp;quot;High Risk of Disengagement.&amp;quot;

Implementation Impact: Instead of hitting the primary operational PostgreSQL database, the system can stream these behavioral events into a high-performance analytical database like ClickHouse. Admins can then be provided with a live Grafana dashboard to visualize department-wide burnout risks before the review cycle even begins.

2. Frictionless Mid-Cycle Reorganizations (The &amp;quot;Matrix&amp;quot; Manager)
The Problem: In large enterprises, teams re-org constantly. If a manager leaves or an employee changes departments mid-quarter, rigid review systems break, leaving orphaned goals and lost feedback.
The Pro Use Case: A seamless &amp;quot;Ownership Transfer&amp;quot; workflow that preserves historical context without breaking the current cycle.

How it works: When a user&amp;#39;s manager_id or department_id is updated in the Auth Service, the system triggers a background job. It freezes the current GoalProgress for the old manager (creating a historical snapshot) and automatically routes the pending ReviewForms to the new manager, appending a &amp;quot;Context Note&amp;quot; containing the old manager&amp;#39;s partial feedback.

Implementation Impact: Running these decoupled event-driven services on a scalable orchestrator like an AWS EKS cluster ensures that massive, org-wide restructuring events don&amp;#39;t lock up the database or delay daily review triggers.

3. The &amp;quot;Zero-Orphan&amp;quot; Goal Calibration Engine
The Problem: Employees often set goals that sound good but do not actually roll up to the company&amp;#39;s strategic objectives, resulting in wasted effort.
The Pro Use Case: A strict, visual top-down calibration system that enforces strategic alignment and mathematically blocks invalid goal states.

How it works: The UI prevents a manager from approving a team goal if it isn&amp;#39;t explicitly linked (parent_id) to a Company Goal. Furthermore, the system enforces a strict 100% weightage rule. If an employee tries to set three goals weighted at 40%, 40%, and 40% (120% total), the API Gateway rejects the payload before it ever hits the Goal Service.

Understanding the Goal Cascade Logic
To make the &amp;quot;Zero-Orphan&amp;quot; calibration work, the system relies on a mathematical cascade. The progress of the entire company is dynamically calculated from the bottom up.

Individual Progress: An employee updates their specific task (e.g., &amp;quot;Deploy new API endpoint&amp;quot;) to 100% complete.

Team Progress Contribution: If that individual goal was weighted at 25% of the overall Team Goal, the Team Goal automatically advances by 25%.

Company Progress Contribution: If that Team Goal makes up 30% of a Company Objective, the top-level executive dashboard automatically updates in real-time.

To visualize how this recursive weightage and progress calculation functions within your proposed architecture, I have generated an interactive simulator below.


Show me the visualization


4. Automated Compliance &amp;amp; Audit Export
The Problem: During legal disputes or HR audits, compiling an employee&amp;#39;s exact state of goals, feedback, and warnings across multiple years is a nightmare.
The Pro Use Case: A one-click &amp;quot;Employee Dossier&amp;quot; generation.

How it works: By leveraging the highly normalized ReviewPerformanceHistory, GoalHistory, and Feedback tables, the Admin UI features an &amp;quot;Export Audit Trail&amp;quot; button. This triggers a background worker queue that compiles a cryptographically hashed, read-only PDF containing every timestamped goal change, cross-shared feedback note, and probation escalation. This guarantees tamper-proof compliance.

# PMS Backend — Implementation Plan

**Scope: Backend only. Do NOT touch frontend.**

**Base: `backend/gms-backend/` (FastAPI + PostgreSQL + SQLAlchemy + Alembic)**

## Current State (What Already Exists)



### ✅ Done

- `User` model — id, email, name, password_hash, role (admin/manager/member), manager_id, team_id

- `Team` model — id, name, manager_id

- `Goal` model — full lifecycle (DRAFT→PENDING_APPROVAL→ACTIVE→AWAITING_FEEDBACK→SCORABLE→SCORED→REJECTED)

- `Subtask` model — goal subtasks with completion tracking

- `Progress` model — completion % updates with notes

- `Feedback` model — member + evaluator feedback with ratings

- `Score` model — final performance rating (below/meets/above expectations)

- Auth — JWT login, bcrypt passwords, HTTPBearer

- RBAC — require_admin, require_manager_or_admin

- Weightage validation — per user per tag period, max 100%

- At-risk detection — &amp;gt;70% time elapsed AND &amp;lt;50% completion

- Repositories — BaseRepository + GoalRepository + UserRepository + TeamRepository

- Services — GoalService, UserService, TeamService, FeedbackService, ScoreService

- Alembic — migration setup with env.py



### ❌ Missing (PRD Requirements)

- `date_of_joining` on User model

- `is_active` on User model

- Probation module (records, triggers, feedback, reminders)

- Review Cycles module (bi-annual + quarterly)

- Review Forms module (self-assessment + manager feedback)

- Notification model + email sending

- Scheduler (APScheduler) for automated triggers

- Red-flag auto-tagging on feedback

- Dashboard aggregation endpoints (team/company stats)

- Reporting endpoints (CSV/PDF export)

- Pagination on list endpoints

- Standardized API response envelope `{status, data, message, timestamp}`

- `parent_id` on Goal for Company→Team→Individual hierarchy

- Goal history/audit log

## Phase 3 — Probation Module

**New files:**

- `app/models/probation.py`

- `app/schemas/probation.py`

- `app/services/probation_service.py`

- `app/routers/probation.py`

- New Alembic migration



### 3.1 Models



#### `ProbationRecord`

```

id, employee_id (FK users), date_of_joining (Date),

probation_status (Enum: IN_PROBATION, COMPLETED, REJECTED, PAUSED),

is_paused (Boolean, default False),

pause_start_date (Date, nullable),

pause_resume_date (Date, nullable),

created_at (DateTime)

```



#### `ProbationTrigger`

```

id, probation_record_id (FK), trigger_day (Integer: 30|60|80),

trigger_date (Date), status (Enum: PENDING, TRIGGERED, SUBMITTED, ESCALATED),

created_at (DateTime)

```



#### `ProbationFeedback`

```

id, probation_trigger_id (FK), submitted_by_id (FK users),

feedback_type (Enum: SELF, MANAGER),

form_data (JSON), submitted_at (DateTime)

```



#### `ProbationReminder`

```

id, probation_trigger_id (FK), reminder_sent_at (DateTime),

reminder_count (Integer, default 0), next_reminder_at (DateTime)

```



### 3.2 Schemas (`app/schemas/probation.py`)

- `ProbationRecordCreate` — employee_id

- `ProbationRecord` — full response with triggers

- `ProbationFeedbackCreate` — form_data (dict), feedback_type

- `ProbationFeedback` — full response

- `ProbationTriggerResponse` — trigger with feedback status



### 3.3 Service (`app/services/probation_service.py`)



#### `calculate_working_days(start_date, end_date) -&amp;gt; int`

- Count Mon–Fri days between dates

- Subtract paused periods



#### `get_or_create_probation_record(db, employee_id) -&amp;gt; ProbationRecord`

- Called when user is created with a DOJ



#### `check_and_fire_triggers(db) -&amp;gt; None` ← called by scheduler

- For each IN_PROBATION record:

- Calculate working days since DOJ (minus paused days)

- For trigger_day in [30, 60, 80]:

- If working_days &amp;gt;= trigger_day AND no trigger exists → create ProbationTrigger (status=TRIGGERED)

- If trigger exists with status=TRIGGERED:

- days since trigger_date &amp;gt;= 2 → send reminder 1

- days since trigger_date &amp;gt;= 4 → send reminder 2

- days since trigger_date &amp;gt;= 6 → send reminder 3

- days since trigger_date &amp;gt;= 7 → escalate (status=ESCALATED), notify admin



#### `submit_probation_feedback(db, trigger_id, user_id, feedback_data) -&amp;gt; ProbationFeedback`

- Validate user is employee (SELF) or manager (MANAGER) of that employee

- Save ProbationFeedback

- If both SELF + MANAGER submitted → update trigger status=SUBMITTED

- Cross-share: both feedbacks become readable to both parties



#### `pause_probation(db, record_id, pause_date) -&amp;gt; ProbationRecord`

#### `resume_probation(db, record_id, resume_date) -&amp;gt; ProbationRecord`

#### `complete_probation(db, record_id) -&amp;gt; ProbationRecord`

#### `reject_probation(db, record_id) -&amp;gt; ProbationRecord`



### 3.4 Router (`app/routers/probation.py`)

```

POST /api/v1/probation/ # Create record (admin)

GET /api/v1/probation/ # List all (admin/manager)

GET /api/v1/probation/{record_id} # Get record detail

GET /api/v1/probation/employee/{employee_id} # Get by employee

POST /api/v1/probation/{record_id}/pause # Pause (admin)

POST /api/v1/probation/{record_id}/resume # Resume (admin)

POST /api/v1/probation/{record_id}/complete # Complete (admin)

POST /api/v1/probation/{record_id}/reject # Reject (admin)

GET /api/v1/probation/triggers/{trigger_id} # Get trigger detail

POST /api/v1/probation/triggers/{trigger_id}/feedback # Submit feedback

GET /api/v1/probation/triggers/{trigger_id}/feedback # Get feedbacks (cross-share)

```



### 3.5 Alembic migration

```bash

alembic revision --autogenerate -m &amp;quot;add_probation_tables&amp;quot;

alembic upgrade head

```

**New files:**

- `app/schemas/review.py`

- `app/services/review_service.py`

- New Alembic migration



### 4.1 Models



#### `ReviewCycle`

```

id, cycle_name (String), cycle_type (Enum: QUARTERLY, BI_ANNUAL),

start_date (Date), end_date (Date),

self_review_deadline (Date), manager_review_deadline (Date),

status (Enum: PENDING, ACTIVE, CLOSED),

created_by_id (FK users), created_at (DateTime)

```



#### `ReviewForm`

```

id, review_cycle_id (FK), employee_id (FK users), manager_id (FK users),

form_type (Enum: SELF_ASSESSMENT, MANAGER_FEEDBACK),

status (Enum: PENDING, IN_PROGRESS, SUBMITTED),

form_data (JSON, nullable), final_rating (Integer 1-5, nullable),

submitted_at (DateTime, nullable), created_at (DateTime)

```



#### `ReviewPerformanceHistory`

```

id, employee_id (FK users), review_cycle_id (FK),

performance_score (Float), rating (String),

feedback_summary (Text), created_at (DateTime)

```



### 4.2 Schemas (`app/schemas/review.py`)

- `ReviewCycleCreate` — cycle_name, cycle_type, start_date, end_date, deadlines

- `ReviewCycle` — full response with form counts

- `ReviewFormSubmit` — form_data (dict), final_rating (optional)

- `ReviewForm` — full response

- `ReviewHistoryResponse` — employee history



### 4.3 Service (`app/services/review_service.py`)



#### `create_cycle(db, cycle_data, created_by_id) -&amp;gt; ReviewCycle`

- Validate dates (end &amp;gt; start, deadlines within range)

- Create ReviewCycle with status=PENDING



#### `trigger_cycle(db, cycle_id, admin_id) -&amp;gt; ReviewCycle`

- Set status=ACTIVE

- For each eligible employee (joined &amp;gt; 60 days before cycle end_date):

- Create ReviewForm (SELF_ASSESSMENT) for employee

- Create ReviewForm (MANAGER_FEEDBACK) for their manager

- Send notifications (stub for now, real email in Phase 6)



#### `submit_form(db, form_id, user_id, form_data, final_rating) -&amp;gt; ReviewForm`

- Validate user owns this form

- Set status=SUBMITTED, submitted_at=now

- If both SELF + MANAGER forms submitted → cross-share + create ReviewPerformanceHistory



#### `close_cycle(db, cycle_id, admin_id) -&amp;gt; ReviewCycle`

- Set status=CLOSED

- Mark all unsubmitted forms as waived (log it)



#### `get_my_forms(db, user_id) -&amp;gt; List[ReviewForm]`

#### `get_cycle_compliance(db, cycle_id) -&amp;gt; dict`

- Returns submitted/total counts per employee

```
POST /api/v1/review-cycles/{cycle_id}/trigger # Trigger cycle (admin)

POST /api/v1/review-cycles/{cycle_id}/close # Close cycle (admin)

GET /api/v1/review-cycles/{cycle_id}/compliance # Compliance report (admin/manager)

GET /api/v1/review-forms/ # Get my forms

GET /api/v1/review-forms/{form_id} # Get form detail

POST /api/v1/review-forms/{form_id}/submit # Submit form

GET /api/v1/review-history/ # My performance history

GET /api/v1/review-history/{employee_id} # Employee history (manager/admin)

```

```bash

alembic revision --autogenerate -m &amp;quot;add_review_tables&amp;quot;

alembic upgrade head

```

## Phase 6 — Scheduler (APScheduler)

**New files:**

- `app/scheduler.py`

- Update `app/main.py`



### 6.1 Install APScheduler

Add to `requirements.txt`:

```

apscheduler==3.10.4

```



### 6.2 `app/scheduler.py`

```python

from apscheduler.schedulers.asyncio import AsyncIOScheduler

from apscheduler.triggers.interval import IntervalTrigger



scheduler = AsyncIOScheduler()



def start_scheduler(app):

scheduler.add_job(

run_probation_checks,

IntervalTrigger(hours=6),

id=&amp;quot;probation_check&amp;quot;,

replace_existing=True

)

scheduler.add_job(

run_review_reminders,

IntervalTrigger(hours=24),

id=&amp;quot;review_reminders&amp;quot;,

replace_existing=True

)

scheduler.start()

```



#### `run_probation_checks()`

- Get DB session

- Call `probation_service.check_and_fire_triggers(db)`

- For each TRIGGERED trigger past reminder thresholds → send reminders/escalations



#### `run_review_reminders()`

- Get DB session

- For each ACTIVE cycle:

- Find unsubmitted forms

- Check days since cycle triggered

- Day 5 → gentle nudge

- Day 15 → urgent nudge

- Day 22 → escalate to admin



### 6.3 Wire into `app/main.py`

```python

@app.on_event(&amp;quot;startup&amp;quot;)

async def startup():

start_scheduler(app)



@app.on_event(&amp;quot;shutdown&amp;quot;)

async def shutdown():

scheduler.shutdown()

```



---

## Phase 10 — Standardized Response Envelope + Pagination

**New file:** `app/response.py`

**Files to change:** all routers (gradual)



### 10.1 `app/response.py`

```python

from datetime import datetime



def success(data, message=&amp;quot;OK&amp;quot;):

return {

&amp;quot;status&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;success&amp;quot;,

&amp;quot;data&amp;quot;: data,

&amp;quot;message&amp;quot;: message,

&amp;quot;timestamp&amp;quot;: datetime.utcnow().isoformat()

}



def error(message, errors=None):

return {

&amp;quot;status&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;error&amp;quot;,

&amp;quot;data&amp;quot;: None,

&amp;quot;message&amp;quot;: message,

&amp;quot;errors&amp;quot;: errors or [],

&amp;quot;timestamp&amp;quot;: datetime.utcnow().isoformat()

}

```



### 10.2 Pagination helper

```python

def paginate(query, page: int, limit: int):

total = query.count()

items = query.offset((page - 1) * limit).limit(limit).all()

return {

&amp;quot;items&amp;quot;: items,

&amp;quot;pagination&amp;quot;: {

&amp;quot;total&amp;quot;: total,

&amp;quot;page&amp;quot;: page,

&amp;quot;pages&amp;quot;: (total + limit - 1) // limit,

&amp;quot;limit&amp;quot;: limit

}

}

```
